架构与源码参考

需要理解程序运行时，再查模块关系和调用链；需要找实现时，查函数索引。 这里是开发过程中的参考资料，不是首次贡献前必须通读的章节。

03 · 总体模块依赖与职责

展开原始细化步骤 / 调用说明
浏览器表单 ──生成脚本──┐
Python op.solve(...) ───┼→ api.build_config → MethodRegistry/参数校验/补丁
CLI → solve/solve_config┘                       ↓ 展开 schema 1 配置
                                          Experiment.run
                       ┌───────────────────────┼────────────────────┐
                    PDEProblem              PINNModel        sampler/formulation
             geometry + residual + BC/IC  feature→net→output       + composer
                       └───────────────────────┼────────────────────┘
                                            Trainer.fit
                                      OptimizerAdapter.step
                                                ↓
                                评价 → ArtifactWriter → ExperimentResult
                                                ↓
                                solve 后处理：标准图 / failure_notes / manifest
                                                ↓ 可选
                                evaluate_reference_csv：独立场对比
文件/模块输入→输出不能混入的职责
api.py用户问题/方法参数→完整配置→ExperimentResult不复制训练循环
config.py路径或 Mapping→独立配置字典不执行 PDE、不自动合并所有目录片段
method_options.py用户选项→校验后的 MethodSelection/有序方法栈不实现数值算法
method_registry.py、methods/*家族/变体→默认补丁、选项补丁、最终配置不持有模型训练状态
registry.py类别+名称+参数→经过类别契约检查的对象不负责方法冲突判定
experiment.py展开配置→组装、训练、评估、产物新算法尽可能放独立公共组件，不继续堆训练分支
geometry.py参数/顶点→域判断、内部/边界采样、法向不读浏览器 DOM
problems.py、conditions.py域+残差+条件→数学问题和误差张量不驱动优化器
autodiff.py、formulations.py坐标/预测→导数/具名点损失与标量损失不借用评价参考场当训练目标
models.py、features.py、constraints.py坐标→特征→网络→约束输出不保存实验文件
sampling.py域及可选模型→SampleBatch不更新模型权重
losses.py具名损失→总目标/自适应权重不替代统一模型优化流程
optimizers.py、training.pyclosure/组件→参数更新、累计历史和状态不自行选择 benchmark
metrics.py、reference_data.py预测与同坐标参考→数值评价不得把参考值流入训练
artifacts.py结果字典/数组→可序列化文件、图、校验清单显示插值不得改原始数组或精度指标

24 · 关键函数按故障反查

现象优先断点/检查函数上游→下游联系
方法名有但运行不支持resolve、validate_method_options、apply_method、_require_componentvariant patch 的组件名必须已注册
参数输入后代码未更新事件 handler、readCustomSettings/readParameter、updateCodeDOM→状态→生成文本，检查 change/input 区别
代码变了但结果没变build_config、apply_method、最终 config.yaml参数可能被后续补丁覆盖，或执行了旧脚本
圆孔条件行正确但图中不显示polygonBoundaryDefinitions、renderCustomPreviewpolygonRing 不应在 polygonEdge 过滤时丢失
导入孔洞顺序变了DXF 环分类、setPolygonData、reconcilePolygonBoundaryState所有 UI/selector 必须引用同一环序
边界零采样sample_boundary、Condition.select、生成 selector几何/容差/采样数共同决定，不是 loss 层应静默处理
二阶导数失败残差函数、grad/hessian、requires_grad检查 detach/no_grad、输出切片维度和常数分支
断点不一致Trainer.state_dict/load_state_dict、sampler/composer 状态不仅模型，还要 active_batch 与 resample 相位
参考指标异常_read_csv、evaluate_reference_csv、metrics列顺序→单位→域检查→分量→样本权重
仅训练成功却没有图调用路径 Experiment.run / solve(plot)标准图属于高层 solve 后处理；不是所有底层调用都出图

附录 B · Python 类与函数逐项索引

范围：src/openpinn、文档扩展、项目顶层示例、用户模板和维护脚本。逐项列出类、方法、嵌套函数、原始签名、源码说明和函数体内调用表达式。没有 docstring 的函数明确标注，不用名称猜测其行为。

这是 AST 静态索引，不是完整运行时调用图。self.xxx、注册工厂、回调与多态对象不作未经验证的目标推断；嵌套函数另列，其调用不计入外层函数。签名默认值的调用、装饰器和匿名 lambda 不属于此处的函数体调用列表。主链路与对象绑定关系请读正文。源码行号只对应本快照。
src/openpinn/__init__.py · 0 个定义

OpenPINN's modular, library-first public API. The short API selects a problem and one or more registered method names while the component API remains available for research extensions. Unsupported, incompatible or conflicting methods fail explicitly rather than silently becoming a baseline. Typical usage:: import openpinn as op result = op.solve("poisson", method="fourier", steps=10) combined = op.solve( "poisson", methods=[op.method("fourier"), op.method("adaptive_weights")], steps=10, )

solve

src/openpinn/api.py:399–504 · function

solve(problem: str | PDEProblem, *, method: str | None=None, variant: str | None=None, methods: Sequence[MethodSelection | Mapping[str, Any]] | None=None, steps: int=1000, seed: int=0, device: str='cpu', dtype: str='float32', method_options: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, problem_options: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, model: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, sampling: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, training: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, overrides: Mapping[str, Any] | None=None, output_dir: str | Path | None=None, output_root: str | Path='runs', run_name: str | None=None, plot: bool=True) → ExperimentResult

Train one built-in or user-defined PDE using method shorthands.

Examples
--------
``solve("poisson", method="fourier", variant="random_fourier", steps=500)``

Independent improvements can be composed with
``methods=[method("fourier"), method("adaptive_weights", "relobralo")]``.
A component-slot conflict is rejected before training starts.

For a new equation, construct :class:`PDEProblem` once and pass that object
in place of the built-in problem name.

函数体调用表达式： ', '.join · (writer.output_dir / 'failure_notes.md').write_text · ArtifactWriter · Experiment · Experiment(config, registry=registry).run · _failure_target · build_config · coerce_method_selection · config.get · default_component_registry · isinstance · registry.register · result.metrics.get · str · write_failure_artifacts · write_result_figures · writer.write_manifest

src/openpinn/autodiff.py · 4 个定义

Small automatic-differentiation helpers for pointwise PINN fields.

from __future__ import annotations
from torch import Tensor

_scalar_component

src/openpinn/autodiff.py:9–22 · function

无独立源码 docstring；结合所属模块正文和调用表达式阅读实现。

函数体调用表达式： IndexError · ValueError · outputs.reshape

grad

src/openpinn/autodiff.py:25–53 · function

grad(outputs: Tensor, inputs: Tensor, component: int | None=None, *, create_graph: bool=True, retain_graph: bool=True) → Tensor

Return pointwise gradients of one output component with respect to inputs.

For the usual PINN batch, each output row depends only on the corresponding
input row. Differentiating the component sum therefore produces an
``(N, D)`` tensor of per-point coordinate derivatives.

函数体调用表达式： ValueError · _scalar_component · torch.autograd.grad · torch.ones_like

hessian

src/openpinn/autodiff.py:56–101 · function

hessian(outputs: Tensor, inputs: Tensor, component: int | None=None, i: int | None=0, j: int | None=None, *, create_graph: bool=True) → Tensor

Return selected second derivatives or a full pointwise Hessian.

``hessian(y, x, i=0, j=0)`` returns shape ``(N, 1)``. Passing
``i=None, j=None`` returns all coordinates with shape ``(N, D, D)``.
If ``j`` is omitted for a selected ``i``, the diagonal derivative ``j=i``
is returned.

函数体调用表达式： ValueError · grad · range · second · torch.cat · torch.stack

hessian.second

src/openpinn/autodiff.py:76–93 · function

无独立源码 docstring；结合所属模块正文和调用表达式阅读实现。

函数体调用表达式： IndexError · torch.autograd.grad · torch.ones_like

BurgersColeHopfReference

src/openpinn/benchmarks.py:17–159 · class

class BurgersColeHopfReference()

Independent Cole–Hopf reference evaluator for the 1D Burgers case.

The transformed heat equation is evaluated with Gauss–Legendre
quadrature and reflected Gaussian images. Computation is deliberately
performed in float64 and in log space so the small default viscosity does
not underflow. This object is an evaluation oracle only: it is never
called by the trainer or loss function.

make_piecewise_poisson_problem

src/openpinn/benchmarks.py:203–268 · function

make_piecewise_poisson_problem(domain: Interval | None=None, *, interface: float=0.0, left_diffusivity: float=1.0, right_diffusivity: float=4.0) → PDEProblem

Build a two-material 1D diffusion benchmark with an exact interface solution.

The problem solves ``-(a(x) u_x)_x = 0`` with ``u(-1)=0`` and
``u(1)=1``. The piecewise-constant coefficient changes at ``interface``.
The analytic field is continuous and has continuous normal flux, while its
slope changes across the material interface. It is a compact
heterogeneous smoke benchmark, not a domain-decomposition implementation.

函数体调用表达式： DirichletBC · Interval · PDEProblem · ValueError · float

make_coupled_manufactured_problem

src/openpinn/benchmarks.py:271–333 · function

make_coupled_manufactured_problem(domain: Interval | None=None, *, coupling_strength: float=1.0) → PDEProblem

Build a two-field elliptic contract case with an explicit coupling term.

The exact fields are ``u=x^2`` and ``v=x^3``. Two coupled balance
equations and the compatible relation ``v_x-3u=0`` are returned as three
separately named residuals. This deliberately overdetermined
manufactured system proves the multi-field extension path; it is not an
engineering-accuracy benchmark.

函数体调用表达式： DirichletBC · Interval · PDEProblem · ValueError · float · math.isfinite

make_burgers_problem

src/openpinn/benchmarks.py:336–401 · function

make_burgers_problem(domain: SpaceTimeDomain | None=None, *, viscosity: float=0.01 / math.pi, nu: float | None=None, reference_quadrature_points: int=1024) → PDEProblem

Build the dynamic 1D viscous Burgers benchmark.

The equation is ``u_t + u*u_x - nu*u_xx = 0`` on
``x in [-1,1], t in [0,1]`` with zero spatial boundary values and initial
state ``-sin(pi*x)``. A Cole–Hopf heat-kernel quadrature supplies an
independent reference field for evaluation; it is not used in training.

函数体调用表达式： BurgersColeHopfReference · DirichletBC · InitialCondition · Interval · PDEProblem · SpaceTimeDomain · ValueError · abs · any · float · math.sin

make_tunnel_consolidation_model1_problem

src/openpinn/benchmarks.py:404–497 · function

make_tunnel_consolidation_model1_problem(*, width_m: float=40.0, height_m: float=40.0, duration_years: float=40.0, tunnel_radius_m: float=4.0, tunnel_centers_m: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...]=((10.0, 20.0), (30.0, 20.0)), initial_pressure_kpa: float=20.0, consolidation_x_m2_per_year: float=0.3, consolidation_y_m2_per_year: float=0.6) → PDEProblem

Build the paper's Model 1 twin-tunnel consolidation PDE.

The network works with the paper's nondimensional variables
``X=x/B``, ``Y=y/H``, ``tau=t/T`` and ``U=u/p0``. It solves
``U_tau - Cx_hat*U_XX - Cy_hat*U_YY = 0`` with ``U=0`` on the outer
rectangle and both tunnel walls, and ``U=1`` initially in the soil.

The Word document fixes the 40 m square, two 4 m-radius tunnels and
40-year horizon. It does not explicitly state Model 1's ``p0``, ``Cx``
or ``Cy`` in that subsection, so those values remain visible parameters;
the defaults are documented assumptions rather than paper ground truth.

函数体调用表达式： DirichletBC · InitialCondition · Interval · PDEProblem · RectangleWithCircularHoles · SpaceTimeDomain · ValueError · any · float · len · math.isclose · math.isfinite · physical_values.values · tuple

HardConstraint

src/openpinn/constraints.py:24–66 · class

class HardConstraint(nn.Module)

Apply the analytic transform ``g(x) + d(x) * network(x)``.

The caller supplies a distance-like function ``d`` that vanishes wherever
the constraint must be exact and an extension ``g`` of the prescribed
value. OpenPINN does not infer a distance function for arbitrary geometry.
Users should verify smoothness and boundary error for their own domain.

HardConstraint.__init__

src/openpinn/constraints.py:33–40 · function

FourierFeatures

src/openpinn/features.py:33–100 · class

class FourierFeatures(nn.Module)

Fixed random Fourier feature mapping.

The mapping is ``[sin(2*pi*xB), cos(2*pi*xB)]`` with an optional copy of
the original coordinates. Frequencies are deterministic for a fixed
seed. This is a feature transform only; the starter does not claim that a
chosen scale is optimal for any PDE.

FormulationProtocol

src/openpinn/formulations.py:18–37 · class

class FormulationProtocol(Protocol)

Structural contract consumed by :class:`openpinn.Trainer`.

A formulation owns the mathematical problem and converts a sampled batch
into named scalar losses. Implementations do not need to inherit from
:class:`StrongFormulation`; satisfying this protocol is sufficient.

FormulationProtocol.problem

src/openpinn/formulations.py:27–30 · function

problem(self) → PDEProblem

StrongFormulation

src/openpinn/formulations.py:40–98 · class

class StrongFormulation()

Pointwise strong-form PINN objective.

This baseline computes mean-square PDE residuals at interior collocation
points and mean-square pointwise errors for every condition. Weak, mixed,
energy and discrete-time forms are deliberately left as future classes.

Ellipse

src/openpinn/geometry.py:437–631 · class

class Ellipse()

A rotated two-dimensional ellipse represented analytically.

``semi_axis_a`` and ``semi_axis_b`` are the two semi-axis lengths. They
are intentionally not reordered, so editing ``a`` and ``b`` in the
configurator has the same meaning as in
``x_local**2 / a**2 + y_local**2 / b**2 = 1``. ``rotation_deg`` rotates
the local ``a`` axis counter-clockwise in physical coordinates.

RoundedRectangle

src/openpinn/geometry.py:635–841 · class

class RoundedRectangle()

An axis-aligned rectangle whose four corners are exact circular arcs.

``corner_radius=0`` gives a rectangle. A square with radius equal to half
its side is a disk, while intermediate values preserve a constant corner
curvature when the width or height is edited.

RectangleWithCircularHoles

src/openpinn/geometry.py:845–1121 · class

class RectangleWithCircularHoles()

An axis-aligned rectangle with non-overlapping circular holes.

Interior points are sampled uniformly by rejection sampling. Boundary
points are sampled uniformly by arc length across the four outer edges and
every circular hole. This geometry is sufficient for the twin-tunnel
consolidation example while remaining independent of that PDE.

RectangleWithCircularHoles.sample_boundary

src/openpinn/geometry.py:965–1045 · function

sample_boundary(self, n: int, *, generator: torch.Generator | None=None, dtype: torch.dtype=torch.float32, device: torch.device | str | None=None) → Tensor

Sample the outer rectangle and all circular perimeters.

By default use the historical arc-length random sampling. With
``stratified_boundary=True``, reserve one point for each of the four
edges and each hole, then allocate the remainder by perimeter length.
This prevents empty selectors for separately prescribed boundaries.

RectangleWithCircularHoles.boundary_normal

src/openpinn/geometry.py:1098–1121 · function

boundary_normal(self, points: Tensor, *, atol: float=1e-05) → Tensor

Outward unit normals; hole normals point into the removed disks.

At rectangle corners use the normalized sum of adjacent edge normals.
Callers prescribing different edge conditions should sample away from
corners, where the smooth-boundary normal is not uniquely defined.

函数体调用表达式： ValueError · length.new_tensor · points.new_tensor · range · self.on_boundary · torch.all · torch.any · torch.isclose · torch.linalg.vector_norm · torch.zeros_like

Polygon

src/openpinn/geometry.py:1125–1446 · class

class Polygon()

A validated two-dimensional polygon with optional polygonal holes.

The outer ring may be convex or concave. Rings are automatically
normalised to counter-clockwise orientation for the outer boundary and
clockwise orientation for holes, so :meth:`boundary_normal` consistently
returns the outward normal of the computational domain. The class has no
optional geometry dependency and can also read CSV, JSON, and GeoJSON
polygon files via :meth:`from_file`.

Polygon.__post_init__

src/openpinn/geometry.py:1139–1165 · function

ClosedSpline

src/openpinn/geometry.py:1450–1726 · class

class ClosedSpline()

A smooth closed cardinal spline controlled by editable points.

Consecutive control points are joined with cubic Bezier segments. The
``tension`` parameter lies in ``[0, 1]``: zero gives the usual smooth
Catmull--Rom curve and increasing it shortens the tangent handles. The
boundary is sampled on the cubic segments and its normal is obtained from
the analytic first derivative; a polyline is used only for inside/outside
tests and never as the represented boundary.

SpaceTimeDomain

src/openpinn/geometry.py:1730–1887 · class

class SpaceTimeDomain()

A Cartesian product of a spatial geometry and a time interval.

Spatial coordinates come first and time is always the last column. Thus
the order is ``(x, t)`` for an interval and ``(x, y, t)`` for a 2D space.

MagnitudePowerLossComposer

src/openpinn/losses.py:58–111 · class

class MagnitudePowerLossComposer(LossComposer)

Compress multi-magnitude loss terms with fixed fractional powers.

This implements only the loss transformation from MMPINN (Wang et al.,
2024, DOI ``10.1016/j.jcp.2024.113112``): ``w_i L_i**(1/r_i)``.
It is a fixed loss-balancing transform, not an adaptive-weight algorithm;
MMPINN's optional multi-level network components are intentionally outside
this minimal one-component variant.

AdaptiveLossComposer

src/openpinn/losses.py:114–234 · class

class AdaptiveLossComposer(nn.Module)

Learn positive term-level weights by ascent while the PINN descends.

This is a deliberately small, independently written term-level variant of
the saddle-point idea in McClenny and Braga-Neto (2023), DOI
``10.1016/j.jcp.2022.111722``. The paper's full SA-PINN uses one mask per
training point; this class instead adds one trainable scalar per named
loss term so it fits OpenPINN's existing scalar loss contract.

PointwiseAdaptiveLossComposer

src/openpinn/losses.py:237–397 · class

class PointwiseAdaptiveLossComposer(nn.Module)

Full pointwise self-adaptive PINN masks with ascent updates.

One positive mask is created for every value returned by
``StrongFormulation.pointwise_loss_terms``. Network parameters descend
the masked objective while masks ascend it, matching the per-collocation
saddle-point structure of McClenny and Braga-Neto (2023), DOI
``10.1016/j.jcp.2022.111722``. The implementation is independent and uses
a monotone softplus mask to keep every coefficient positive.

PointwiseAdaptiveLossComposer.__init__

UncertaintyLossComposer

src/openpinn/losses.py:400–501 · class

class UncertaintyLossComposer(nn.Module)

Balance scalar PINN terms with learned homoscedastic uncertainties.

The effective coefficient is ``exp(-s_i)`` and ``s_i = log(sigma_i^2)``.
The network minimizes the weighted losses while the uncertainty variables
minimize ``sum(exp(-s_i) * L_i + s_i)`` using detached loss values. This
is independently implemented from Kendall et al. (2018), arXiv:1705.07115,
and the AMAW-PINN formulation of Hou et al. (2023).

ReLoBRaLoLossComposer

src/openpinn/losses.py:504–638 · class

class ReLoBRaLoLossComposer(nn.Module)

Relative loss balancing with random lookback (ReLoBRaLo).

This is an independent implementation of the update equations in Bischof
and Kraus, *Multi-Objective Loss Balancing for Physics-Informed Deep
Learning*. The balancing weights are detached state, not model
parameters, and their random stream is checkpointed for reproducibility.

User-facing method selections and parameter metadata. This module is the single catalogue used by the short Python API and the documentation configurator. A parameter listed here is editable through ``op.method(...)``; unknown names fail before training starts.

src/openpinn/method_registry.py · 25 个定义

High-level method names mapped to the components they configure. The numerical implementations remain in their responsibility modules (``models.py``, ``features.py``, ``sampling.py`` and so on). This module is only the small switchboard that lets users select one method by name without copying a training program.

r2_score

src/openpinn/metrics.py:52–66 · function

r2_score(prediction: Any, target: Any, *, eps: float=1e-12) → float

Coefficient of determination with a finite constant-target convention.

A constant target has no variance. In that case an exact prediction is
assigned 1.0 and every non-exact prediction 0.0, matching the finite-force
convention commonly used by metric libraries.

函数体调用表达式： (1.0 - residual_sum / total_sum).cpu · (pred - ref).square · (ref - torch.mean(ref)).square · _tensor · _tensor(target).to · float · residual_sum.detach · residual_sum.detach().cpu · torch.mean · torch.sum · total_sum.detach · total_sum.detach().cpu

AdaptiveTanh

src/openpinn/models.py:15–34 · class

class AdaptiveTanh(nn.Module)

Tanh with one trainable positive slope per hidden layer.

This minimal layer-wise variant follows the trainable-activation idea in
Jagtap, Kawaguchi and Karniadakis (2020), DOI
``10.1016/j.jcp.2019.109136``. It is an independent PyTorch
implementation and does not copy the MIT-licensed TensorFlow reference
implementation.

ModifiedMLP

src/openpinn/models.py:115–199 · class

class ModifiedMLP(nn.Module)

Gated MLP proposed for mitigating PINN gradient pathologies.

This is an independent PyTorch implementation of the architecture in
Wang, Teng and Perdikaris (2021), DOI ``10.1137/20M1318043``. It does not
copy code from the paper's reference repository, whose license was not
explicit when the OpenPINN handover was prepared.

All hidden layers use one width because the two input encoders are blended
elementwise with every hidden state.

DomainDecompositionPINN

src/openpinn/models.py:202–261 · class

class DomainDecompositionPINN(nn.Module)

Two independent MLPs joined at one 1D spatial interface.

The accompanying interface formulation supplies solution and flux
continuity losses. This is an independent minimal two-subdomain design
inspired by XPINNs, DOI ``10.4208/cicp.OA-2020-0164``; no reference code
is copied.

freeze_all_but_last_linear_layers

src/openpinn/models.py:316–339 · function

freeze_all_but_last_linear_layers(module: nn.Module, last_n: int=1) → None

Freeze a module except for its last ``last_n`` linear layers.

Checkpoint loading changes tensor values but not ``requires_grad`` flags, so
this helper can be applied when a model is built and remain effective after
a strict model-only warm start.

src/openpinn/physics.py · 18 个定义

Validated single-field physical expressions, evaluated without code execution. Geometry and boundary values retain their input units. Inside a residual, coordinates, the field and parameters are converted to SI. ``dx``/``dy``/``dt`` differentiate with respect to the SI coordinate (chain rule included). Returned residuals are in SI; this is dimensional validation, not automatic loss scaling. Multi-field poromechanics and flux boundary conditions are not implemented here.

导入依赖（含条件 / 局部导入）

validate_physics_spec(spec: Mapping[str, Any]) → dict[str, Any]

Validate a pure-JSON scalar expression and return an isolated copy.

Coordinates must use length/time units, with equal x/y units. Bare numbers
are dimensionless; use a named parameter for a dimensional constant.
``expression_source`` is optional display text, never executed.

PDEProblem

src/openpinn/problems.py:26–176 · class

class PDEProblem()

Bundle a domain, PDE residual and boundary/initial conditions.

Parameters
----------
domain:
Geometry on which the equation is solved.
residual:
Callable ``residual(coordinates, prediction)``. It may return one
tensor or a mapping of named residual tensors for multi-equation PDEs.
conditions:
Dirichlet and/or initial constraints.
exact_solution:
Optional callable used only for evaluation, never for training.

src/openpinn/reference_data.py · 6 个定义

Evaluate a saved PINN model against an independent tabular reference field. Reference rows are never supplied to the optimizer. Coordinates are evaluated directly at the exported sample locations; no interpolation or training-grid reuse is hidden in the reported errors.

evaluate_reference_csv

src/openpinn/reference_data.py:192–328 · function

evaluate_reference_csv(result: ExperimentResult, path: str | Path, *, coordinate_columns: Sequence[str | int]=('x',), value_column: str | int='u', problem: PDEProblem | None=None, field_index: int=0, coordinate_scales: Sequence[float] | None=None, value_scale: float=1.0, prediction_scale: float=1.0, time_axis: bool | None=None, fixed_time: float | None=None, batch_size: int=4096) → dict[str, float]

Compare an independent CSV/COMSOL Spreadsheet field and save artifacts.

``coordinate_columns`` must follow model input order (for example x,y,t).
Named headers and 1-based selectors such as ``"#1"`` are supported.
A COMSOL Spreadsheet table may have ``%`` comment lines and no header;
comma, semicolon, tab and whitespace separators are recognized.
For one exported time slice without a time column, pass spatial columns
and ``fixed_time`` in the exported time unit; its scale is still the last
entry of ``coordinate_scales``.
Coordinate scales convert exported coordinates into model input units.
``value_scale`` and ``prediction_scale`` independently convert the
exported and model values into the same *reported* physical unit.
All supplied coordinates must belong to ``problem.domain`` when supplied;
out-of-domain samples fail loudly instead of biasing metrics by filtering.

函数体调用表达式： (writer.output_dir / 'reference_comparison.svg').write_text · (writer.output_dir / 'reference_predictions.csv').open · (~in_domain).sum · (~in_domain).sum().item · ArtifactWriter · FloatingPointError · Path · Path(path).expanduser · Path(path).expanduser().resolve · ValueError · _comparison_svg · _read_csv · abs · bool · csv.writer · csv_writer.writerow · hasattr · hashlib.sha256 · hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest · int · json.loads · json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')).get · len · manifest_path.is_file · manifest_path.read_text · math.isfinite · max_absolute_error · mean_absolute_error · metrics.items · names[-1].strip · next · np.all · np.any · np.asarray · np.column_stack · np.concatenate · np.full · np.isfinite · np.ones · output[:, field_index].detach · output[:, field_index].detach().cpu · output[:, field_index].detach().cpu().numpy · output[:, field_index].detach().cpu().numpy().astype · predictions.append · problem.domain.contains · r2_score · range · re.match · relative_l2_error · result.metrics.update · result.model · result.model.eval · result.model.parameters · result.model.train · rmse · scales.tolist · source.read_bytes · str · torch.as_tensor · torch.device · torch.inference_mode · tuple · writer.write_json · writer.write_manifest · writer.write_metrics · zip

src/openpinn/registry.py · 12 个定义

Typed component factories for configuration-driven construction. The registry is intentionally small: it maps a component *category* and a normalized name to one factory, then validates the factory result against the category contract. It is not a global Trick manager and contains no training or experiment orchestration logic.

导入依赖（含条件 / 局部导入）

ComponentRegistry

src/openpinn/registry.py:63–225 · class

class ComponentRegistry()

Category-separated registry with duplicate and result-type checks.

Registries are explicit objects rather than mutable process globals. Use
:func:`openpinn.default_component_registry` to obtain an isolated registry
containing the built-in components, then register project-specific
factories on that object and pass it to :class:`openpinn.Experiment`.

ComponentRegistry.register

src/openpinn/registry.py:110–162 · function

register(self, kind: ComponentKind, name: str, factory: ComponentFactory, *, aliases: Iterable[str]=(), replace: bool=False) → None

Register one factory and optional aliases inside one category.

Duplicate names and aliases are rejected unless ``replace=True``.
Replacing a canonical entry removes all aliases of the previous entry,
so stale aliases cannot continue constructing the old factory.

函数体调用表达式： ', '.join · TypeError · ValueError · _ComponentSpec · alias_map.items · alias_names.add · callable · normalize_component_name · self._kind · set · sorted · specs.get · tuple

SamplerProtocol

src/openpinn/sampling.py:58–86 · class

class SamplerProtocol(Protocol)

Structural contract consumed by :class:`openpinn.Trainer`.

Stateful samplers expose checkpoint state in addition to producing a
:class:`SampleBatch`. Adaptive and curriculum samplers can implement this
contract without inheriting from :class:`UniformSampler`.

UniformSampler

src/openpinn/sampling.py:89–203 · class

class UniformSampler()

Uniformly sample interior, spatial-boundary and initial points.

The sampler is deliberately static and simple. Residual-adaptive sampling
is an advanced method and should be implemented as a separately tested
extension rather than being implied by this class.

ResidualAdaptiveSampler

src/openpinn/sampling.py:206–407 · class

class ResidualAdaptiveSampler()

Resample interior points from a residual-weighted candidate pool.

This independent minimal RAD-style sampler follows the residual-based
distribution idea in Wu et al. (2023), DOI
``10.1016/j.cma.2022.115671``. Boundary and initial points remain uniform,
and the number of interior points never changes.

AdaptiveMovementSampler

src/openpinn/sampling.py:410–651 · class

class AdaptiveMovementSampler()

Keep part of the collocation set fixed and move the remaining points.

This independently written implementation follows the RAM/WAM probability
definitions in Hou et al. (2023), DOI ``10.1007/s11071-023-08654-w``.
``indicator='residual'`` selects RAM; ``indicator='solution_gradient'``
selects WAM. The total number of training points stays constant.

ImportanceSamplingSampler

src/openpinn/sampling.py:654–850 · class

class ImportanceSamplingSampler()

Sample PDE points by loss and apply inverse-probability correction.

The finite candidate-pool estimator follows Nabian et al. (2021), DOI
``10.1111/mice.12685``: points are sampled with probability proportional
to their current PDE loss, and each selected contribution receives
``1 / (N q_i)`` so the mini-batch mean remains an unbiased estimator of
the candidate-pool mean. Boundary and initial losses remain uniform.

docs/_ext/openpinn_search.py · 13 个定义

Index rendered documentation, including raw-HTML manuals, for offline search. The index is a local script, not a fetched JSON file: file:// browsers can load it. Rebuild from all current source pages after writing HTML, including incremental builds, so deleted pages and unchanged pages are handled without a second cache.

导入依赖（含条件 / 局部导入）

from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import quote

import re

scripts/build_docs.py · 1 个定义
Build current manuals, validate examples, build Sphinx, then package manuals.

Cross-platform canonical documentation entry point. Does not run the full test
suite or claim visual browser validation. Any failed command stops publication
of the archive; older output files may remain and must not be treated as current.

scripts/validate_method_configurator.py · 3 个定义
Short-run every code option exposed by the documentation configurator.

Validation outputs are intentionally kept under ``results/`` rather than in
the method implementation folders.  The script uses the same public
``op.method(...)`` calls and parameter defaults that the web page renders.
